\displaystyle\leq cH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{2SAHd}{\delta}}{n}},

\displaystyle\max_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}}|V_{h}^{*}(s,y;\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{*}_{h}(s,y;\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})|

\displaystyle\leq cH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{2SAHd}{\delta}}{n}}.

where
c
is some positive constant.

Proof.

First, we observe that, for any
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}
, by following passages similar
to those in the proof of Lemma
E.7
:

\displaystyle|V_{h}^{*}(s,y;\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{\psi^{*}}_{h}(s,y;\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})|

\displaystyle\qquad=|Q_{h}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\psi^{*}}}(s,y,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\psi^{*}_{h}(s,y)};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-Q^{\psi^{*}}_{h}(s,y,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\psi^{*}_{h}(s,y)};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})|

\displaystyle\qquad=\Big|\mathfrak{r}_{h}(s,y,\psi^{*}_{h}(s,y))+\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\mathfrak{p}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,\psi^{*}_{h}(s,y))V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\Big(\mathfrak{r}_{h}(s,y,\psi^{*}_{h}(s,y))+\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\widehat{\mathfrak{p}}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,\psi^{*}_{h}(s,y))V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad=\Big|\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\mathfrak{p}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,\psi^{*}_{h}(s,y))V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\widehat{\mathfrak{p}}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,\psi^{*}_{h}(s,y))V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\widehat{\mathfrak{p}}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,\psi^{*}_{h}(s,y))V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})}\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad=\Big|\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\Big(\mathfrak{p}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,\psi^{*}_{h}(s,y))-\widehat{\mathfrak{p}}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,\psi^{*}_{h}(s,y))\Big)V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\widehat{\mathfrak{p}}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,\psi^{*}_{h}(s,y))\Big(V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad=\Big|{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,\psi^{*}_{h}(s,y))-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,\psi^{*}_{h}(s,y))\Big)V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,\psi^{*}_{h}(s,y));\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\widehat{\mathfrak{p}}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,\psi^{*}_{h}(s,y))\Big(V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad=\dotsc

\displaystyle\qquad=\bigg|\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r},\psi^{*}}\bigg[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-\widehat{p}_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})\Big)V_{h^{\prime}+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y_{h^{\prime}+1};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\Big|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y\bigg]\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{\leq}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r},\psi^{*}}\bigg[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bigg|}\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-\widehat{p}_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})\Big)V_{h^{\prime}+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y_{h^{\prime}+1};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r}){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bigg|}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\bigg|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y\bigg],

where at (1) we have brought the absolute value inside the expectation.

Similarly, for the other term, for any
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}
, we can write:

\displaystyle|V_{h}^{*}(s,y;\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{*}_{h}(s,y;\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})|

\displaystyle\qquad=|V_{h}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\psi^{*}}}(s,y;\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\widehat{\psi}^{*}}}_{h}(s,y;\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})|

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{=}|{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\max\limits_{a\in\mathcal{A}}}Q_{h}^{\psi^{*}}(s,y,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}a};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\max\limits_{a\in\mathcal{A}}}Q^{\widehat{\psi}^{*}}_{h}(s,y,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}a};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})|

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{\leq}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\max\limits_{a\in\mathcal{A}}}|Q_{h}^{\psi^{*}}(s,y,a;\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-Q^{\widehat{\psi}^{*}}_{h}(s,y,a;\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})|

\displaystyle\qquad=\max\limits_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\Big|\mathfrak{r}_{h}(s,y,a)+\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\mathfrak{p}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,a)V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\Big(\mathfrak{r}_{h}(s,y,a)+\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\widehat{\mathfrak{p}}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,a)V_{h+1}^{\widehat{\psi}^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad=\max\limits_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\Big|\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\mathfrak{p}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,a)V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\widehat{\mathfrak{p}}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,a)V_{h+1}^{\widehat{\psi}^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\widehat{\mathfrak{p}}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,a)V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})}\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad=\max\limits_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\Big|\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\Big(\mathfrak{p}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,a)-\widehat{\mathfrak{p}}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,a)\Big)V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\widehat{\mathfrak{p}}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,a)\Big(V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V_{h+1}^{\widehat{\psi}^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{\leq}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\Big|}\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\Big(\mathfrak{p}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\overline{a}})-\widehat{\mathfrak{p}}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\overline{a}})\Big)V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r}){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\Big|}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\Big|}\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\widehat{\mathfrak{p}}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\overline{a}})\Big(V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V_{h+1}^{\widehat{\psi}^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})\Big){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\Big|}

\displaystyle\qquad=\Big|{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,\overline{a})-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,\overline{a})\Big)V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y+\Pi_{\mathcal{R}}[r_{h}(s,\overline{a})];\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})}\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\Big|\sum\limits_{(s^{\prime},y^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h+1}}\widehat{\mathfrak{p}}_{h}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime}|s,y,\overline{a})\Big(V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V_{h+1}^{\widehat{\psi}^{*}}(s^{\prime},y^{\prime};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\dotsc

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(5)}}}{\leq}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\overline{\psi}}}\bigg[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\bigg|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-\widehat{p}_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})\Big)V_{h^{\prime}+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y_{h^{\prime}+1};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\bigg|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y\bigg],

where at (2) we have applied the Bellman optimality equation, at (3) we have
upper bounded the difference of maxima with the maximum of the difference, at
(4) we denote the maximal action by
\overline{a}
, and we apply triangle
inequality; at (5) we have unfolded the recursion and called
\overline{\psi}

the resulting policy.

Now, for some
\epsilon\in(0,1)
, let us denote by
\mathcal{E}
the event defined as:

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}\coloneqq\bigg\{

\displaystyle\forall h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}:

\displaystyle\qquad\big|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\Big)V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a);\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\big|\leq\epsilon\bigg\}

We can write:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{\complement})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\bigg(\exists h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}:

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\big|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\Big)V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a);\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\big|>\epsilon\bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(6)}}}{\leq}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\mathbb{P}\bigg(\big|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\Big)V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a);\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\big|>\epsilon\bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(7)}}}{\leq}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}}2e^{\frac{-2n\epsilon^{2}}{H^{2}}}

\displaystyle=2SAHde^{\frac{-2n\epsilon^{2}}{H^{2}}},

where at (6) we have applied a union bound over all tuples

h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}
, and at (7) we have applied Hoeffding’s inequality, by
recalling that we collect
n
samples (see Algorithm
19
) for any
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\llbracket H\rrbracket
triple, and that vector

V_{h+1}^{\psi^{*}}(\cdot,y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a);\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})
bounded by
[0,H]
is
independent of the randomness in
\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)
. It should be
remarked that our collection of samples depends only on
\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\llbracket H\rrbracket
, and not on

\mathcal{Y}_{h}
; such term enters the expression only through the union bound, because
we have to apply Hoeffding’s inequality for all the value functions considered,
which are as many as
|\mathcal{Y}_{h}|
. Note that we use
d=|\mathcal{Y}_{H+1}|
since it is
the largest
|\mathcal{Y}_{h}|
among
h\in\llbracket H+1\rrbracket
.

This probability is at most
\delta
if:

\displaystyle 2SAHde^{\frac{-2n\epsilon^{2}}{H^{2}}}\leq\delta\iff\epsilon\geq H\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{2SAHd}{\delta}}{2n}}.

By plugging into the previous expressions, we obtain that, w.p.
1-\delta
:

\displaystyle|V_{h}^{*}(s,y;\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{\psi^{*}}_{h}(s,y;\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r},\psi^{*}}\bigg[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\bigg|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-\widehat{p}_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})\Big)V_{h^{\prime}+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y_{h^{\prime}+1};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\bigg|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r},\psi^{*}}\bigg[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}H\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{2SAHd}{\delta}}{2n}}\,\bigg|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad=H^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{2SAHd}{\delta}}{2n}},

and also:

\displaystyle|V_{h}^{*}(s,y;\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{*}_{h}(s,y;\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r},\overline{\psi}}\bigg[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\bigg|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-\widehat{p}_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})\Big)V_{h^{\prime}+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y_{h^{\prime}+1};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\bigg|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r},\overline{\psi}}\bigg[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}H\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{2SAHd}{\delta}}{2n}}\,\bigg|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad=H^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{2SAHd}{\delta}}{2n}}.

This concludes the proof.

∎

Lemma E.9
.

For any
\delta\in(0,1)
, w.p. at least
1-\delta
, it holds that, for all
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}
:

\displaystyle\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a);\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]}\leq

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a);\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}+b_{1},

\displaystyle\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a);\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]}\leq

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a);\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}+b_{1},

where
b_{1}
is defined as:

\displaystyle b_{1}\coloneqq cH\bigg(\frac{\log\frac{4SAHY}{\delta}}{n}\bigg)^{1/4}+c^{\prime}H^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{4SAHY}{\delta}}{n}},

for some positive constants
c,c^{\prime}
.

Proof.

In the following, we will use
\overline{y}
as a label for
y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a)
.
We begin with the first expression. We can write, for any
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]}

\displaystyle\qquad=\Big(\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]-\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\Big)V^{*^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}2}}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\Big[\Big(\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big)^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\Big(\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big)^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}2}}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r}){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})}]

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{=}\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\Big)V^{*^{2}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\Big[\Big(\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\Big(\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big)^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}+\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}+2\text{Cov}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r}),}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{\leq}\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\Big)V^{*^{2}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\Big[\Big(\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\Big(\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big)^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\Big(\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\cdot\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]\Big)^{1/2}}

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\Big)V^{*^{2}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\Big[\Big(\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\Big(\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big)^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\Big[\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}+\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}\Big]^{2}},

where at (1) we have used the common formula for the variance

\mathbb{V}[X]=\mathbb{E}[X^{2}]-\mathbb{E}[X]^{2}
,
at (2) we have decomposed the variance of a sum as
\mathbb{V}[X+Y]=\mathbb{V}[X]+\mathbb{V}[Y]+2\text{Cov}[X,Y]
,
at (3) we have applied Cauchy-Schwarz’s inequality to bound the covariance with
the product of the variances
|\text{Cov}[X,Y]|\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{V}[X]\mathbb{V}[Y]}
.

Next, observe that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{=}\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[(V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r}))^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}2}}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(5)}}}{\leq}\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[(V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r}))^{2}]

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(6)}}}{\leq}\|(V^{*}_{h+1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\cdot},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\cdot},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r}))^{2}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad=\|V^{*}_{h+1}(\cdot,\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(\cdot,\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})\|_{\infty}^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}2}},

where at (4) we have used
\mathbb{V}[X]=\mathbb{E}[X^{2}]-\mathbb{E}[X]^{2}
, at (5) we recognize that the
second term is a square, thus always positive, and we remove it, and at (6) we
have upper bounded the expected value, an average, through the infinity norm.

Thanks to this expression, we can continue to upper bound the previous term
as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\Big)V^{*^{2}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\Big[\Big(\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\Big(\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big)^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\Big[{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\|V^{*}_{h+1}(\cdot,\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(\cdot,\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})\|_{\infty}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}\Big]^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(7)}}}{=}\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\Big)V^{*^{2}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\Big[{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\Big(\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a))V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\cdot\Big(\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)+\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a))V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big)}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\Big[\|V^{*}_{h+1}(\cdot,\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(\cdot,\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}\Big]^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(8)}}}{\leq}\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\Big)V^{*^{2}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\Big[\Big(\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a))V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\cdot\Big(\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)+\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a))V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\Big[{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}cH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{4SAHd}{\delta}}{n}}}+\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}\Big]^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(9)}}}{\leq}\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\Big)V^{*^{2}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}2H\Big|}\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a))V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r}){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\Big|}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\Big[cH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{4SAHd}{\delta}}{n}}+\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}\Big]^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(10)}}}{\leq}\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\Big)V^{*^{2}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}2cH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{4SAHd}{\delta}}{n}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\Big[cH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{4SAHd}{\delta}}{n}}+\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}\Big]^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(11)}}}{\leq}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}cH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{4SAHd}{\delta}}{n}}}+2cH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{4SAHd}{\delta}}{n}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\Big[cH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{4SAHd}{\delta}}{n}}+\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}\Big]^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad={\color[rgb]{0,0,1}3}cH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{4SAHd}{\delta}}{n}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\Big[cH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{4SAHd}{\delta}}{n}}+\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}\Big]^{2},

where at (7) we have applied the common formula
x^{2}-y^{2}=(x-y)(x+y)
, at (8) we
have applied Lemma
E.8
using probability
\delta^{\prime}=\delta/2
,
and noticing that, for how the discretized MDP is constructed, we have that

\overline{y}\in\mathcal{Y}
, at (9) we have upper bounded the second term with the
absolute value and recognized that the value function does not exceed
H
and
the sum of probabilities is no greater than
2
; at (10) we recognize that, in
the proof of Lemma
E.8
, we had already bounded that term, thus,
under the event
\mathcal{E}
which holds w.p.
1-\delta/2
, we have that bound; at (11)
we have applied Hoeffding’s inequality to all tuples
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}
with
probability
\delta/(2SAHd)
, and noticed that the square of the value function does
not exceed
H^{2}
.

Observe that the previous formula holds for all
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}
w.p.

1-\delta
(by summing the two
\delta/2
through a union bound). By taking the
square root of both sides, we obtain:

\displaystyle\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\Big(3cH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{4SAHd}{\delta}}{n}}+\Big[cH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{4SAHd}{\delta}}{n}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}\Big]^{2}\Big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(12)}}}{\leq}\underbrace{c^{\prime}H\sqrt[4]{\frac{\log\frac{4SAHY}{\delta}}{n}}+cH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{4SAHY}{\delta}}{n}}}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\eqqcolon b_{1}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}

\displaystyle\qquad=\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}+b_{1},

where at (12) we have used the fact that
\sqrt{a+b}\leq\sqrt{a}+\sqrt{b}
.

To prove the second formula, the passages are basically the same, the only
difference is that, at passage (1), we sum and subtract

V^{\widehat{\psi}^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})
instead of

V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})
, and that at passage (8) we
apply the other expression in Lemma
E.8
. This concludes the
proof.
∎

Lemma E.10
.

For any
\delta\in(0,1)
, define:

\displaystyle c_{1}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\log\frac{2SAHd}{\delta},

\displaystyle b_{2}

\displaystyle\coloneqq cH\bigg(\frac{\log\frac{8SAHd}{\delta}}{n}\bigg)^{3/4}+c^{\prime}H^{2}\frac{\log\frac{8SAHd}{\delta}}{n},

for some positive constants
c,c^{\prime}
.
Then, w.p. at least
1-\delta
, we have, for all
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}
:

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\Big)V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a);\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\leq c^{\prime\prime}\sqrt{\frac{c_{1}\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a);\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}{n}}+b_{2},

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\Big)V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a);\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\geq-c^{\prime\prime\prime}\sqrt{\frac{c_{1}\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a);\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}{n}}+b_{2},

for some positive constants
c^{\prime\prime},c^{\prime\prime\prime}
.

Proof.

Again, we will write
\overline{y}
instead of
y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a)
for simplicity.
For all
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}
, we can write:

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\Big)V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{\leq}\sqrt{\frac{2\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{*}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]\log\frac{2SAHd}{\delta}}{n}}+\frac{2H\log\frac{2SAHd}{\delta}}{3n}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\sqrt{\frac{2\log\frac{2SAHd}{\delta}}{n}}\Big(\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\widehat{p}}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\psi^{*}}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\widehat{\mathfrak{p}}},R)]}+b_{1}\Big)+\frac{2H\log\frac{2SAHd}{\delta}}{3n}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{=}c\sqrt{\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}c_{1}}\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}{n}}+c^{\prime}\sqrt{\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}c_{1}}}{n}}H\bigg(\frac{\log\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}8}SAHd}{\delta}}{n}\bigg)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+c^{\prime\prime}\sqrt{\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}c_{1}}}{n}}H^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}8}SAHd}{\delta}}{n}}+c^{\prime\prime\prime}H\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}c_{1}}}{n}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq c\sqrt{\frac{c_{1}\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi^{*}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})]}{n}}+c^{\prime}H\bigg(\frac{\log\frac{8SAHd}{\delta}}{n}\bigg)^{3/4}+c^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}H^{2}\frac{\log\frac{8SAHd}{\delta}}{n},

where at (1) we have applied the Bernstein’s inequality using
\delta/(2SAHd)

as probability for all
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}
, and at (2) we have applied Lemma

E.9
with
\delta/2
of probability, and a union bound to
guarantee the event to hold w.p.
1-\delta
, at (3) we use the definition of

c_{1}\coloneqq\log\frac{2SAHd}{\delta}
, and denoted by
c,c^{\prime},c^{\prime\prime},c^{\prime\prime\prime}
some
positive constants.

For the other expression, an analogous derivation can be carried out. In
particular, we use the other side of the Bernstein’s inequality, and the other
expression in Lemma
E.9
.
∎

Lemma E.11
.

For any
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}
and deterministic policy
\psi
, let
\Sigma^{\psi}_{h}(s,y,a)
be defined as:

\displaystyle\Sigma^{\psi}_{h}(s,y,a)\coloneqq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r},\psi}\Big[\Big|\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\mathfrak{r}_{h^{\prime}}(s_{h^{\prime}},y_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-Q^{\psi}_{h}(s,y,a;\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big|^{2}\,|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y,a_{h}=a\Big].

Then, function
\Sigma
satisfies the Bellman equation, i.e., for any

h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}
and deterministic policy
\psi
:

\displaystyle\Sigma^{\psi}_{h}(s,y,a)=

\displaystyle\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a);\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]

\displaystyle+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[\Sigma^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a),\psi_{h+1}(s^{\prime},y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a)))].

Proof.

For all
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}
and deterministic policy
\psi
, we can write (we
denote
a^{\prime}\coloneqq\psi_{h+1}(s^{\prime},y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a))
and

\overline{y}\coloneqq y+\overline{r}_{h}(s,a)
for notational simplicity, and
we remark that
\overline{y}
is
not
a random variable):

\displaystyle\Sigma^{\psi}_{h}(s,y,a)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r},\psi}\Big[\Big|\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\mathfrak{r}_{h^{\prime}}(s_{h^{\prime}},y_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-Q^{\psi}_{h}(s,y,a;\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big|^{2}\,|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y,a_{h}=a\Big]

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}}\bigg[\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r},\psi}\Big[\Big|\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\mathfrak{r}_{h^{\prime}}(s_{h^{\prime}},y_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}-Q^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y},a^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})}

\displaystyle\qquad-\big(Q^{\psi}_{h}(s,y,a;\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r}){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}-Q^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y},a^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})}\big)\Big|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad|\,s_{h}=s,a_{h}=a,y_{h}=y,s_{h+1}=s^{\prime}\Big]\bigg]

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{=}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}\bigg[\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r},\psi}\Big[\Big|\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}={\color[rgb]{0,0,1}h+1}}^{H}\mathfrak{r}_{h^{\prime}}(s_{h^{\prime}},y_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-Q^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y},a^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})

\displaystyle\qquad-\big(Q^{\psi}_{h}(s,y,a;\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\mathfrak{r}_{h}(s,y,a)}-Q^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y},a^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\big)\Big|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad|\,s_{h+1}=s^{\prime},y_{h+1}=\overline{y}\Big]\bigg]

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{=}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}\bigg[\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r},\psi}\Big[\Big|\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h+1}^{H}\mathfrak{r}_{h^{\prime}}(s_{h^{\prime}},y_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-Q^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y},a^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad|\,s_{h+1}=s^{\prime},y_{h+1}=\overline{y}\Big]\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad-2\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}\bigg[\big(Q^{\psi}_{h}(s,y,a;\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-\mathfrak{r}_{h}(s,y,a)-Q^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y},a^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\underbrace{\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r},\psi}\Big[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h+1}^{H}\mathfrak{r}_{h^{\prime}}(s_{h^{\prime}},y_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-Q^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y},a^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\,\big|\,s_{h+1}=s^{\prime},y_{h+1}=\overline{y}\Big]}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}=0}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}\Big[\big|Q^{\psi}_{h}(s,y,a;\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-\mathfrak{r}_{h}(s,y,a)-Q^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y},a^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\big|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{=}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}\bigg[

\displaystyle\qquad\underbrace{\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r},\psi}\Big[\Big|\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h+1}^{H}\mathfrak{r}_{h^{\prime}}(s_{h^{\prime}},y_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-Q^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y},a^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\Big|^{2}\,|\,s_{h+1}=s^{\prime},y_{h+1}=\overline{y}\Big]}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}=\Sigma^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y},a^{\prime})}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\underbrace{\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}\Big[\big|Q^{\psi}_{h}(s,y,a;\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-\mathfrak{r}_{h}(s,y,a)-Q^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y},a^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\big|^{2}\Big]}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\eqqcolon\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[Q^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y},a^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]=\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]}}

\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[\Sigma^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y},a^{\prime})]+\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}[V^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})],

at (1) we add and subtract a term, at (2) we bring out the non-random reward
received at
h
, at (3) we compute the square and use the linearity of
expectation, at (4) we use the fact that
\mathbb{E}_{\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r},\psi}\big[\sum_{h^{\prime}=h+1}^{H}\mathfrak{r}_{h^{\prime}}(s_{h^{\prime}},y_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-Q^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y},a^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\,\big|\,s_{h+1}=s^{\prime}\big]=Q^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y},a^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-Q^{\psi}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},\overline{y},a^{\prime};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})=0
because of linearity of
expectation.
∎

Lemma E.12
.

Let
\psi
be any policy, and let
\mathfrak{p}
be any
transition model associated to an arbitrary inner dynamics
p
. Then, for all

h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r},\psi}\bigg[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})}[V^{\psi}_{h^{\prime}+1}(s^{\prime},y_{h^{\prime}+1};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]}\,\bigg|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y,a_{h}=a\bigg]\bigg|\leq\sqrt{H^{3}}.

Proof.

For all
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}
, we can write (note that this derivation is
independent of
\mathfrak{p},p
, so we might use even
\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\widehat{p}
in the
proof):

\displaystyle\Big|\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r},\psi}\Big[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\sqrt{\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})}[V^{\psi}_{h^{\prime}+1}(s^{\prime},y_{h^{\prime}+1};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]}\,|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y,a_{h}=a\Big]\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{\leq}\Big|\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r},\psi}\Big[\sqrt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}H\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}}\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})}[V^{\psi}_{h^{\prime}+1}(s^{\prime},y_{h^{\prime}+1};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]}\,|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y,a_{h}=a\Big]\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\sqrt{H}}\sqrt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r},\psi}}\Big[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\mathbb{V}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})}[V^{\psi}_{h^{\prime}+1}(s^{\prime},y_{h^{\prime}+1};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})]\,|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y,a_{h}=a\Big]}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{=}\sqrt{H}\bigg(\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r},\psi}\Big[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\Sigma^{\psi}_{h^{\prime}}(s_{h^{\prime}},y_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})}\big[\Sigma^{\psi}_{h^{\prime}+1}(s^{\prime},y_{h^{\prime}+1},\psi_{h^{\prime}+1}(s^{\prime},y_{h^{\prime}+1}))\big]}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y,a_{h}=a\Big]\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad=\sqrt{H}\sqrt{\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r},\psi}\Big[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\Sigma^{\psi}_{h^{\prime}}(s_{h^{\prime}},y_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\Sigma^{\psi}_{h^{\prime}+1}(s_{h^{\prime}+1},y_{h^{\prime}+1},a_{h^{\prime}+1})}\,|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y,a_{h}=a\Big]}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{=}\sqrt{H}\sqrt{\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r},\psi}\Big[{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\Sigma^{\psi}_{h}(s_{h},y_{h},a_{h})-\underbrace{\Sigma^{\psi}_{H+1}(s_{H+1},y_{H+1},a_{H+1})}_{=0}}\,|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y,a_{h}=a\Big]}

\displaystyle\qquad=\sqrt{H}\sqrt{\Sigma^{\psi}_{h}(s,y,a)}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(5)}}}{\leq}\sqrt{H}\sqrt{H^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad=\sqrt{H^{3}},

where at (1) we have applied the Cauchy-Schwarz’s inequality, at (2) we have
applied Jensen’s inequality, at (3) we have applied Lemma
E.11
, at (4) we have used telescoping, and at (5) we have bounded

\Sigma^{\psi}_{h}(s,y,a)\leq H^{2}
for all
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}
.
∎

E.4.3
Lemmas on the Optimal Performance for Multiple Utilities

To prove the following results, we will make use of the notation introduced in
the previous section.

Lemma E.13
.

Let
\epsilon,\delta\in(0,1)
. It suffices to execute
CATY-UL
with:

\displaystyle\tau\leq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\Big(\frac{SAH^{5}}{\epsilon^{2}}\Big(S+\log\frac{SAH}{\delta}\Big)\Big),

to obtain
\sup_{U\in\mathfrak{U}_{L}}\big|J^{*}(U;p,r)-\widehat{J}^{*}(U)\big|\leq HL\epsilon_{0}+\epsilon

w.p.
1-\delta
.

Proof.

Similarly to the proof of Lemma
E.13
, we
can write:

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{U\in\mathfrak{U}_{L}}

\displaystyle|J^{*}(U;p,r)-\widehat{J}^{*}(U)|

\displaystyle=\sup\limits_{U\in\mathfrak{U}_{L}}|J^{*}(U;p,r)-\widehat{J}^{*}(U){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm J^{*}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})}|

\displaystyle\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\sup\limits_{U\in\mathfrak{U}_{L}}|}J^{*}(U;p,r)-J^{*}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r}){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}|}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\sup\limits_{U\in\mathfrak{U}_{L}}|}J^{*}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-\widehat{J}^{*}(U){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}|}

\displaystyle=\sup\limits_{U\in\mathfrak{U}_{L}}|J^{*}(U;p,r)-J^{*}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})|+\sup\limits_{U\in\mathfrak{U}_{L}}|J^{*}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}J^{*}(\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})}|

\displaystyle\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}HL\epsilon_{0}}+\sup\limits_{U\in\mathfrak{U}_{L}}|J^{*}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-J^{*}(\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{\leq}HL\epsilon_{0}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}H^{2}\sqrt{\frac{2}{n}\Big(\log\frac{SAH}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\Big)}}

\displaystyle\leq HL\epsilon_{0}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\epsilon},

where at (1) we have applied the formula in Lemma
E.14
.

By enforcing such quantity to be smaller than
\epsilon
, we get:

\displaystyle H^{2}\sqrt{\frac{2}{n}\Big(\log\frac{SAH}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\Big)}\leq

\displaystyle\qquad\frac{H^{2}\sqrt{\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{2\Big(\log\frac{SAH}{\delta}+(S-1)\Big)}\leq\epsilon

\displaystyle\qquad\iff n\geq 2\frac{H^{4}}{\epsilon^{2}}\Big(\log\frac{SAH}{\delta}+(S-1)\Big)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big).

By summing over all
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\llbracket H\rrbracket
,
and
by applying Lemma J.3 of
Lazzati et al. (2024b)
,
we obtain that:

\displaystyle\tau=SAHn\geq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\bigg(\frac{SAH^{5}}{\epsilon^{2}}\Big(\log\frac{SAH}{\delta}+S\Big)\bigg).

∎

Lemma E.14
.

For any
\delta\in(0,1)
, for
all utility functions
U\in\mathfrak{U}_{L}
at the same time, we have:

\displaystyle|J^{*}_{h}(\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-J^{*}_{h}(\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})|\leq H^{2}\sqrt{\frac{2}{n}\Big(\log\frac{SAH}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\Big)},

w.p. at least
1-\delta
.

Proof.

Let us denote by
\mathcal{E}
the event defined as:

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}\coloneqq\bigg\{

\displaystyle\forall n\in\mathbb{N},\,\forall h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}:

\displaystyle n\text{KL}\big(\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)\|p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)\big)\leq\log\frac{SAH}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\bigg\}.

We can write:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{\complement})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\bigg(\exists n\in\mathbb{N},\,\exists h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\times\mathcal{A}:

\displaystyle\qquad n\text{KL}\big(\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)\|p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)\big)>\log\frac{SAH}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\mathbb{P}\bigg(\exists n\in\mathbb{N},\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\exists(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\llbracket H\rrbracket}:

\displaystyle\qquad n\text{KL}\big(\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)\|p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)\big)>\log\frac{SAH}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\sum\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\llbracket H\rrbracket}\mathbb{P}\bigg(\exists n\in\mathbb{N},\,n\text{KL}\big(\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)\|p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)\big)>

\displaystyle\qquad\log\frac{SAH}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{\leq}\sum\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\llbracket H\rrbracket}\frac{\delta}{SAH}

\displaystyle\leq\delta,

where at (1) we realize that there is no dependence on variable
y
, thus we can
drop it,
9
9

9

Therefore, differently from the event for a single utility,
now there is no dependence on
d
in the bound. Intuitively,
d
appeared in the
case of a single utility because we had to apply Hoeffding’s inequality
d

times, because we had, potentially,
d
different value functions (as many as
the states). Since now we provide the bound for all the possible value functions
(1-norm bound), then the dependence on
d
disappears.

at (2) we have applied a union bound over all triples
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\llbracket H\rrbracket
,
and at (3) we have applied Proposition 1 of

Jonsson et al. (2020)
.

Next, for all utilities
U\in\mathfrak{U}_{L}
at the same time, for all the tuples

h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket,(s,y)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}
, we can write:

\displaystyle|V_{h}^{*}(s,y;\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})-V^{*}_{h}(s,y;\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r})|

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{\leq}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r},\overline{\psi}}\bigg[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\bigg|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-\widehat{p}_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})\Big)V_{h^{\prime}+1}^{\psi^{*}}(s^{\prime},y_{h^{\prime}+1};\mathfrak{p},\mathfrak{r})\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\Big|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y,a_{h}=a\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(5)}}}{\leq}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}H}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r},\overline{\psi}}\bigg[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\|}p_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-\widehat{p}_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\|_{1}}\,\Big|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y,a_{h}=a\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(6)}}}{\leq}H\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r},\overline{\psi}}\bigg[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\sqrt{2\text{KL}(\widehat{p}_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})\|p_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}}))}}\,\Big|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y,a_{h}=a\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(7)}}}{\leq}H\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\widehat{\mathfrak{p}},\mathfrak{r},\overline{\psi}}\bigg[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\sqrt{\frac{2}{n}\Big(\log\frac{SAH}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\Big)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\Big|\,s_{h}=s,y_{h}=y,a_{h}=a\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad={\color[rgb]{0,0,1}H^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{2}{n}\Big(\log\frac{SAH}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\Big)},

where at (4) we apply the formula derived in the proof of Lemma
E.8
and triangle inequality, at (5) we have upper bounded with the 1-norm,
defined as
\|f\|_{1}\coloneqq\sum_{x}|f(x)|
, at (6) we have applied Pinsker’s
inequality, at (7) we assume that concentration event
\mathcal{E}
holds.

We remark that the guarantee provided by this theorem holds not only for

L
-Lipschitz utilities, but for all functions with the same dimensionality
(since it is a bound in 1-norm).
∎

E.5
Analysis of
TRACTOR-UL

See
5.2

Proof.

The proof draws inspiration from those of

Syed & Schapire (2007)
and
Schlaginhaufen & Kamgarpour (2024)
.

Given any distribution
\eta
supported on
\mathcal{Y}
, and given any two utilities

U\in\underline{\mathfrak{U}}_{L},\overline{U}\in\overline{\underline{\mathfrak{U}}}_{L}
(where
U

is a function on
[0,H]
and
\overline{U}
is a vector on
\mathcal{Y}
), we will
abuse notation and write both
U^{\intercal}\eta
and
\overline{U}^{\intercal}\eta
, with obvious meaning.

Moreover, for
L>0
, we define operator
\mathfrak{C}_{L}:\overline{\underline{\mathfrak{U}}}_{L}\to 2^{\underline{\mathfrak{U}}_{L}}
(where
2^{\mathcal{X}}
denotes the power set of set
\mathcal{X}
)
that, given vector
\overline{U}\in\overline{\underline{\mathfrak{U}}}_{L}
, returns the
set
\mathfrak{C}_{L}(\overline{U})\coloneqq\{U\in\underline{\mathfrak{U}}_{L}\,|\,\forall y\in\mathcal{Y}:\,U(y)=\overline{U}(y)\}
.

First of all, we observe that the guarantee provided by the theorem follows
directly by the following expression:

\displaystyle\mathop{\mathbb{P}}\limits_{\mathcal{M}^{1},\mathcal{M}^{2},\dotsc,\mathcal{M}^{N}}\Big(\sup\limits_{U\in\mathfrak{C}_{L}(\widehat{U})}\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}\overline{\mathcal{C}}_{p^{i},r^{i},\pi^{E,i}}(U)\leq\epsilon\Big)\geq 1-\delta,

where
\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{M}^{1},\mathcal{M}^{2},\dotsc,\mathcal{M}^{N}}
denotes the joint
probability distribution obtained by the
N
MDPs
\{\mathcal{M}^{i}\}_{i}
.

Let us denote by
\widehat{U}\coloneqq(\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\overline{U}_{t})/T
the output of

TRACTOR-UL
. Note that
\widehat{U}\in\overline{\underline{\mathfrak{U}}}_{L}
. We can
write:

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{U\in\mathfrak{C}_{L}(\widehat{U})}\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}\overline{\mathcal{C}}_{p^{i},r^{i},\pi^{E,i}}(U)

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\sup\limits_{U\in\mathfrak{C}_{L}(\widehat{U})}\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}\bigg(J^{*}(U;p^{i},r^{i})-J^{\pi^{E,i}}(U;p^{i},r^{i}){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm\widehat{U}^{\intercal}\widehat{\eta}^{E,i}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\sup\limits_{U\in\mathfrak{C}_{L}(\widehat{U})}\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}\bigg(J^{*}(U;p^{i},r^{i})-{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\widehat{U}^{\intercal}\widehat{\eta}^{E,i}}\bigg)+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\epsilon_{1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{=}\sup\limits_{U\in\mathfrak{C}_{L}(\widehat{U})}\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}\bigg({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\max\limits_{\eta\in\mathfrak{D}_{i}}U^{\intercal}\eta}-\widehat{U}^{\intercal}\widehat{\eta}^{E,i}\bigg)+\epsilon_{1}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{=}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\sup\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}U_{0}\in\mathfrak{C}_{L}(\overline{U}_{0}),\\
\dotsc,\\
U_{T-1}\in\mathfrak{C}_{L}(\overline{U}_{T-1})\end{subarray}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\frac{1}{T}}\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}\max\limits_{\eta\in\mathfrak{D}_{i}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\sum\limits_{t=0}^{T-1}}\bigg({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}U_{t}}^{\intercal}\eta-{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\overline{U}_{t}}^{\intercal}\widehat{\eta}^{E,i}\bigg)+\epsilon_{1}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(5)}}}{\leq}\frac{1}{T}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\sum\limits_{t=0}^{T-1}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\sup\limits_{U_{t}\in\mathfrak{C}_{L}(\overline{U}_{t})}}\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}\bigg(\max\limits_{\eta\in\mathfrak{D}_{i}}U_{t}^{\intercal}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm\overline{U}_{t}^{\intercal}\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}}-\overline{U}_{t}^{\intercal}\widehat{\eta}^{E,i}\bigg)+\epsilon_{1}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(6)}}}{\leq}\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=0}^{T-1}\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}\overline{U}_{t}^{\intercal}\Big(\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}-\widehat{\eta}^{E,i}\Big){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm\frac{1}{T}\min\limits_{\overline{U}\in\overline{\underline{\mathfrak{U}}}_{L}}\sum\limits_{t=0}^{T-1}\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}\overline{U}^{\intercal}\Big(\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}-\widehat{\eta}^{E,i}\Big)}+\epsilon_{1}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\epsilon_{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(7)}}}{\leq}\frac{1}{T}\min\limits_{\overline{U}\in\overline{\underline{\mathfrak{U}}}_{L}}\sum\limits_{t=0}^{T-1}\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}\overline{U}^{\intercal}\Big(\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}-\widehat{\eta}^{E,i}\Big)+\epsilon_{1}+\epsilon_{2}+\underbrace{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\frac{2HN\sqrt{H/\epsilon_{0}}}{\sqrt{T}}}}_{\eqqcolon\epsilon_{3}}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(8)}}}{\leq}\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=0}^{T-1}\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\overline{U}^{E,\intercal}}\Big(\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}-\widehat{\eta}^{E,i}\Big){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm U^{E,\intercal}\eta^{p^{i},r^{i},\pi^{E,i}}}+\epsilon_{1}+\epsilon_{2}+\epsilon_{3}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(9)}}}{\leq}\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=0}^{T-1}\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}\overline{U}^{E,\intercal}\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm U^{E,\intercal}\eta^{p^{i},r^{i},\overline{\pi}^{i}_{t}}}-U^{E,\intercal}\eta^{p^{i},r^{i},\pi^{E,i}}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}2}\epsilon_{1}+\epsilon_{2}+\epsilon_{3}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(10)}}}{\leq}\frac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=0}^{T-1}\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}\underbrace{U^{E,\intercal}\Big(\eta^{p^{i},r^{i},\overline{\pi}^{i}_{t}}-\eta^{p^{i},r^{i},\pi^{E,i}}\Big)}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\leq 0}}+2\epsilon_{1}+\epsilon_{2}+\epsilon_{3}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\epsilon_{4}}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(11)}}}{\leq}2\epsilon_{1}+\epsilon_{2}+\epsilon_{3}+\epsilon_{4},

where
at (1) we apply the definition of (non)compatibility, at (2) we first
upper bound the supremum of a sum with the sum of the supremum, and
then we apply Lemma
E.15
w.p.
\delta/3
,
and denote
\epsilon_{1}\coloneqq NL\sqrt{2H\epsilon_{0}}+\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}cH\sqrt{\frac{H\log\frac{NH\tau^{E,i}}{\delta\epsilon_{0}}}{\epsilon_{0}\tau^{E,i}}}
, at (3) we denote by

\mathfrak{D}_{i}
the set of possible return distributions in environment
i
, at
(4) we use the definition of
\widehat{U}
, and realize that all
functions
U\in\mathfrak{C}_{L}(\widehat{U})
can be constructed based on
T

functions
U_{0}\in\mathfrak{C}_{L}(\overline{U}_{0}),\dotsc,U_{T-1}\in\mathfrak{C}_{L}(\overline{U}_{T-1})
. At (5) we upper bound the
maximum of the sum with the sum of maxima, and exchange the two
summations, and we add and subtract the dot product between the
(discretized) utility
U_{t}
and the estimate of the return distribution
computed at Line
8
; moreover, we
bring the sup inside the summation. At (6) we upper bound the supremum
of the sum with the sum of the supremum, and we apply Lemma
E.16
w.p.
\delta/3
, defining

\epsilon_{2}\coloneqq cNH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\Big(\log\frac{SAHN}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\Big)}+NHL\epsilon_{0}+c^{\prime}HN\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{NT}{\delta}}{K}}
,
and we add and subtract a term, at (7) we apply Theorem H.2 from

Schlaginhaufen & Kamgarpour (2024)
since set

\overline{\underline{\mathfrak{U}}}_{L}
is closed and convex, where
D\coloneqq\max_{\overline{U},\overline{U}^{\prime}\in\overline{\underline{\mathfrak{U}}}_{L}}\|\overline{U}-\overline{U}^{\prime}\|_{2}=\sqrt{d-2}H=\sqrt{\lfloor H/\epsilon_{0}\rfloor-1}H\leq H\sqrt{H/\epsilon_{0}}
(recall that we
consider increasing and not strictly-increasing utilities),
10
10

10

The
maximum is attained by discretized utilities

\overline{U},\overline{U}^{\prime}
that assign, respectively,

\overline{U}(y)=0
and
\overline{U}^{\prime}(y)=H
to all the

y\in\mathcal{Y}\setminus\{y_{1},y_{d}\}
.
and

\max_{\overline{U}\in\overline{\underline{\mathfrak{U}}}_{L}}\|\nabla\sum_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}\overline{U}^{\intercal}(\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}-\widehat{\eta}^{E,i})\|_{2}=\|\sum_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}-\widehat{\eta}^{E,i}\|_{2}\leq\sum_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}\|\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}\|_{1}+\|\widehat{\eta}^{E,i}\|_{1}=2N\eqqcolon G

(because
\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}
and
\widehat{\eta}^{E,i}
are probability
distributions), with learning rate

\alpha=D/(G\sqrt{T})=H\sqrt{d-2}/(2N\sqrt{T})=\sqrt{\lfloor H/\epsilon_{0}\rfloor-1}H/(2N\sqrt{T})
,
at (8) we upper bound the minimum over utilities with a specific choice
of utility,
\overline{U}^{E}
, and we add and subtract a term; note that

\overline{U}^{E}\in\overline{\underline{\mathfrak{U}}}_{L}
corresponds to the
expert’s utility
U^{E}\in\underline{\mathfrak{U}}_{L}
(by hypothesis), i.e., for
all
y\in\mathcal{Y}:\,\overline{U}^{E}(y)=U^{E}(y)
. Note that, by hypothesis,

U^{E}
makes all the expert policies optimal, i.e.,
\forall i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket:\,U^{E,\intercal}\eta^{p^{i},r^{i},\pi^{E,i}}=\sup_{\pi}U^{E,\intercal}\eta^{p^{i},r^{i},\pi}
.
At (9) we note that, under the good event of Lemma
E.15
, we can provide an upper bound using the term in Lemma

E.15
(since
U^{E}\in\underline{\mathfrak{U}}_{L}
); in
addition, we sum and subtract a term that depends on some policy

\overline{\pi}^{i}_{t}
, whose existence is guaranteed by Lemma
E.17
, which
we apply at the next step.
At (10) we apply Lemma
E.17
w.p.
\delta/3
, and we define as

\epsilon_{4}
the upper bound times
N
.
Finally, at (11) we use the hypothesis that utility
U^{E}

makes the expert policy optimal in all environments.

We want that
2\epsilon_{1}+\epsilon_{2}+\epsilon_{3}+\epsilon_{4}\leq\epsilon
.
We can rewrite the sum as:

\displaystyle 2\epsilon_{1}+\epsilon_{2}+\epsilon_{3}+\epsilon_{4}

\displaystyle\qquad=\Big(2NL\sqrt{2H\epsilon_{0}}+\frac{3}{2}LNH\epsilon_{0}\Big)+c\frac{HN\sqrt{H}}{\sqrt{\epsilon_{0}T}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+c^{\prime}\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}H\sqrt{\frac{H\log\frac{NH\tau^{E,i}}{\delta\epsilon_{0}}}{\epsilon_{0}\tau^{E,i}}}+c^{\prime\prime}NH\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{NT}{\delta}}{K}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+c^{\prime\prime\prime}NH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\Big(\log\frac{SAHN}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\Big)}.

By imposing each term smaller than
\epsilon/5
, we find that it
suffices that

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\epsilon_{0}=\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{80N^{2}L^{2}H}\\
T\geq\mathcal{O}\Big(\frac{N^{2}H^{3}}{\epsilon_{0}\epsilon^{2}}\Big)\geq\mathcal{O}\Big(\frac{N^{4}H^{4}L^{2}}{\epsilon^{4}}\Big)\\
\tau^{E,i}\geq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\Big(\frac{H^{3}N^{2}\log\frac{NH}{\delta\epsilon_{0}}}{\epsilon_{0}\epsilon^{2}}\Big)\geq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\Big(\frac{H^{4}N^{4}L^{2}\log\frac{NHL}{\delta\epsilon}}{\epsilon^{4}}\Big)\qquad\forall i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket\\
K\geq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\Big(\frac{N^{2}H^{2}\log\frac{NT}{\delta}}{\epsilon^{2}}\Big)\geq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\Big(\frac{N^{2}H^{2}\log\frac{NHL}{\delta\epsilon}}{\epsilon^{2}}\Big)\\
\tau^{i}\geq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\Big(\frac{N^{2}SAH^{5}}{\epsilon^{2}}\Big(S+\log\frac{SAHN}{\delta}\Big)\qquad\forall i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket\end{cases},

where we have used that
\tau^{i}=SAHn
for all
i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket
, and also
used Lemma J.3 of
Lazzati et al. (2024b)
.

The statement of the theorem follows through the application of a union bound.
∎

Lemma E.15
.

Let
\delta\in(0,1)
.
Then, it holds that, w.p. at least
1-\delta
:

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{U\in\underline{\mathfrak{U}}_{L}}\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}\bigg|U^{\intercal}\widehat{\eta}^{E,i}-J^{\pi^{E,i}}(U;p^{i},r^{i})\bigg|\leq NL\sqrt{2H\epsilon_{0}}+\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}cH\sqrt{\frac{H\log\frac{NH\tau^{E,i}}{\delta\epsilon_{0}}}{\epsilon_{0}\tau^{E,i}}},

where
c
is some positive constant.

Proof.

We can make the same derivation as in the proof of Theorem
5.1
to upper bound the objective with the sum of two terms, which
can then be bounded using Lemma
E.1
and the expression
(Eq. (
17
)) obtained in the proof of
Lemma
E.4
w.p.
\delta/N
:

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{U\in\underline{\mathfrak{U}}_{L}}\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}\bigg|U^{\intercal}\widehat{\eta}^{E,i}-J^{\pi^{E,i}}(U;p^{i},r^{i})\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq L\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}w_{1}(\eta^{p^{i},r^{i},\pi^{E,i}},\text{Proj}_{\mathcal{C}}(\eta^{p^{i},r^{i},\pi^{E,i}}))

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\sup\limits_{\overline{U}^{\prime}\in[0,H]^{d}}}\big|\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{G\sim\text{Proj}_{\mathcal{C}}(\eta^{p^{i},r^{i},\pi^{E,i}})}[\overline{U}^{\prime}(G)]-\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{G\sim\widehat{\eta}^{E,i}}[\overline{U}^{\prime}(G)]\big|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq LN\sqrt{2H\epsilon_{0}}+\sum\limits_{i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket}cH\sqrt{\frac{H\log\frac{NH\tau^{E,i}}{\delta\epsilon_{0}}}{\epsilon_{0}\tau^{E,i}}}.

The result follows through the application of the union bound.
∎

Lemma E.16
.

Let
\delta\in(0,1)
. With
probability at least
1-\delta
, for all
t\in\{0,1,\dotsc,T-1\}
, for all

i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{U_{t}\in\mathfrak{C}_{L}(\overline{U}_{t})}\max\limits_{\eta\in\mathfrak{D}_{i}}U_{t}^{\intercal}\eta-\overline{U}_{t}^{\intercal}\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq cH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\Big(\log\frac{SAHN}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\Big)}

\displaystyle\qquad+HL\epsilon_{0}+c^{\prime}H\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{NT}{\delta}}{K}},

where
c,c^{\prime}
are some positive constants.

Proof.

We use the notation in Section
5
. In
particular,
let
policy
\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}
be the optimal policy in the RS-MDP

\widehat{\mathcal{M}}^{i}_{\overline{U}_{t}}\coloneqq(\mathcal{S}^{i},\mathcal{A}^{i},H,s_{0}^{i},\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\overline{U}_{t})
, i.e.:

\displaystyle J^{\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}(\overline{U}_{t};\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i})=J^{*}(\overline{U}_{t};\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i})=J^{*}(U_{t};\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i}),

where the last passage holds trivially for all
U_{t}\in\mathfrak{C}_{L}(\overline{U}_{t})

(because there is no evaluation of utility outside
\mathcal{Y}
).

Thus,
for all
t\in\{0,1,\dotsc,T-1\}
, we have:

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{U_{t}\in\mathfrak{C}_{L}(\overline{U}_{t})}\max\limits_{\eta\in\mathfrak{D}_{i}}U_{t}^{\intercal}\eta-\overline{U}_{t}^{\intercal}\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm J^{*}(U_{t};\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i})}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{\leq}\sup\limits_{U_{t}\in\mathfrak{C}_{L}(\overline{U}_{t})}\Big|J^{*}(U_{t};p^{i},r^{i})-J^{*}(U_{t};\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i})\Big|+\Big|{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\overline{U}_{t}}^{\intercal}\Big(\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\eta^{\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}}\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}HL\epsilon_{0}+cH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\Big(\log\frac{SAHN}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\Big)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\Big|\overline{U}_{t}^{\intercal}\Big(\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}-\eta^{\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{\leq}HL\epsilon_{0}+cH^{2}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\Big(\log\frac{SAHN}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\Big)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+c^{\prime}H\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{NT}{\delta}}{K}},

where at (1) we have applied the triangle inequality, and realized that in the
second term there is no dependence on the value of utility outside of
\mathcal{Y}
;
moreover, we have used that
J^{*}(U_{t};\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i})=\overline{U}_{t}^{\intercal}\eta^{\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}
by definition of
policy
\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}
.
At (2) we apply Lemma
E.13
(our

J^{*}(U_{t};\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i})
has the same meaning of

\widehat{J}^{*}(U)
in the lemma, and we upper bound
\sup_{U_{t}\in\mathfrak{C}_{L}(\overline{U}_{t})}
with
\sup_{U\in\underline{\mathfrak{U}}_{L}}
) w.p.

\delta/(2N)
,
11
11

11

We remark that, in doing so, we can still apply
Proposition 3 of
Wu & Xu (2023)
inside the proof of Lemma

E.13
even though we consider

increasing
utilities instead of
strictly-increasing
utilities;
indeed, it is trivial to observe that the proof of Proposition 3 of

Wu & Xu (2023)
does not depend on such property.
and we keep the
confidence bound explicit, and we upper bound
d\leq H/\epsilon_{0}+1
,
and at (3) we observe that
\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}
is the empirical estimate of
distribution
\eta^{\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}
(see
Line
8
) obtained through the sampling of

K
sample returns
G_{1},G_{2},\dotsc,G_{K}\overset{\text{i.i.d.}}{\sim}\eta^{\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}
. Indeed, note that
the policy
\widehat{\psi}^{*,i}_{t}
, computed at Line
8
and optimal for

\mathfrak{E}[\widehat{\mathcal{M}}^{i}_{\overline{U}_{t}}]=(\{\mathcal{S}^{i}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\}_{h},\mathcal{A}^{i},H,s_{0}^{i},\widehat{\mathfrak{p}}^{i},\mathfrak{r}^{i}_{t})
,
12
12

12

See Section
2
for
the meaning of
\widehat{\mathfrak{p}}^{i}
and
\mathfrak{r}^{i}_{t}
; we use
\mathcal{Y}_{h}
for all
h
in
the state space instead of the sets of partial returns

\{\mathcal{G}^{\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i}}_{h}\}_{h}
in order to obtain policy

\widehat{\psi}^{*,i}_{t}
supported on the entire
\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}
space, and to
make it compliant with Algorithm
4
provides policy

\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}
through the formula in Section
2
, thus Line
8
is actually simulating

\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}
in MDP
\widehat{\mathcal{M}}^{i}
. Therefore, we can apply
Hoeffding’s inequality (e.g., see Lemma
E.3
) w.p.
\delta/(2TN)
.

The result follows through the application of the union bound.

We remark that in one case we use probability
\delta/(2N)
(without
T
)
while in the other we use
\delta/(2NT)
(with
T
), because in the former we
provide a guarantee for all possible utilities w.r.t. the optimal performance,
thus all the
T
steps are already included; instead, in the latter, we
provide a guarantee for a single utility and for a single policy at a specific

t\in\{0,\dotsc,T-1\}
, thus we have to compute a union bound with
T
.
∎

Lemma E.17
.

Let
\delta\in(0,1)
. With
probability at least
1-\delta
, for all
i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket
and
t\in\{0,\dotsc,T-1\}
, under
the good event in Lemma
E.16
, there exists a
policy
\overline{\pi}^{i}_{t}
such that:

\displaystyle\overline{U}^{E,\intercal}\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}-U^{E,\intercal}\eta^{p^{i},r^{i},\overline{\pi}^{i}}

\displaystyle\leq LH\epsilon_{0}/2+cH\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{NT}{\delta}}{K}}

\displaystyle\qquad+c^{\prime}H^{2}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\Big(\log\frac{SAHN}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\Big)},

where
c,c^{\prime}
are positive constants.

Proof.

First, simply observe that
\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}
is the empirical estimate (see
Line
8
) of

\eta^{\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}
, thus,
similarly to the proof of Lemma
E.16
, for all
i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket

and
t\in\{0,1,\dotsc,T-1\}
, we can apply Hoeffding’s inequality w.p.
\delta/(2TN)
:

\displaystyle\Big|\overline{U}^{E,\intercal}\Big(\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}-\eta^{\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}\Big)\Big|\leq cH\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{NT}{\delta}}{K}}.

Now, we compare distributions

\eta^{\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}
and

\eta^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p^{i}},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}
.
Through straightforward passages, we can write:

\displaystyle|U^{E,\intercal}\Big(\eta^{\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}-\eta^{p^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}\Big)|

\displaystyle\qquad=|J^{\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}(\overline{U}^{E};\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i})-J^{\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}(\overline{U}^{E};p^{i},\overline{r}^{i})|

\displaystyle\qquad=\Big|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p^{i}_{1}(s^{\prime}|s_{0}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t,1}(s_{0}^{i}))V_{2}^{\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}(s^{\prime};p^{i},\overline{r}^{i})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{p}^{i}_{1}(s^{\prime}|s_{0}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t,1}(s_{0}^{i}))V_{2}^{\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}(s^{\prime};\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i})\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\bigg|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p^{i}_{1}(s^{\prime}|s_{0}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t,1}(s_{0}^{i}))-\widehat{p}^{i}_{1}(s^{\prime}|s_{0}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t,1}(s_{0}^{i}))\Big)V_{2}^{\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}(s^{\prime};p^{i},\overline{r}^{i})\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widehat{p}^{i}_{1}(s^{\prime}|s_{0}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t,1}(s_{0}^{i},0))\Big|V_{2}^{\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}(s^{\prime};p^{i},\overline{r}^{i})-V_{2}^{\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}(s^{\prime};\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i})\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\dotsc

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}\bigg[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=1}^{H}\bigg|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Big(p^{i}_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-\widehat{p}^{i}_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime}|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})\Big)V_{h^{\prime}+1}^{\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}(s^{\prime};p^{i},\overline{r}^{i})\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\bigg|\,s_{1}=s_{0}^{i}\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}H}\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}\bigg[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=1}^{H}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\Big\|}p^{i}_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})-\widehat{p}^{i}_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\Big\|_{1}}\bigg|\,s_{1}=s_{0}^{i}\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq H\mathop{\mathbb{E}}\limits_{\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}\bigg[\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}=1}^{H}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\sqrt{2\text{KL}(p^{i}_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}})\|\widehat{p}^{i}_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h^{\prime}},a_{h^{\prime}}))}}\bigg|\,s_{1}=s_{0}^{i}\bigg],

where at the last passage we applied the Pinsker’s inequality.
Note that the previous derivation was possible as long as
as policy
\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}
is defined over all the possible pairs
state-cumulative reward
(s,y)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}
for all
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket
.
Since we construct it through policy
\widehat{\psi}^{*,i}_{t}
, obtained at
Line
8
, i.e., over the entire enlarged state space

\{\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{Y}_{h}\}_{h}
, then policy
\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}
satsifies such
property.
Now, in the proof of Lemma
E.16
we used Lemma

E.14
, in which event
\mathcal{E}
bounds the
KL-divergence between transition models. Therefore, under the application of
Lemma
E.16
, it holds that:

\displaystyle|U^{E,\intercal}\Big(\eta^{\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}-\eta^{p^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}\Big)|\leq H^{2}\sqrt{\frac{2}{n}\Big(\log\frac{SAHN}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\Big)},

where
n
is the number of samples takes at each
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\llbracket H\rrbracket
in the

i\in\llbracket N\rrbracket
MDP.

Therefore, we can finally write:

\displaystyle\overline{U}^{E,\intercal}\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}-U^{E,\intercal}\eta^{p^{i},r^{i},\overline{\pi}^{i}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm\overline{U}^{E,\intercal}\eta^{\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}\pm\overline{U}^{E,\intercal}\eta^{p^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}}

\displaystyle\qquad=U^{E,\intercal}\Big(\eta^{p^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}-\eta^{p^{i},r^{i},\overline{\pi}^{i}}\Big)+\overline{U}^{E,\intercal}\Big(\eta^{\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}-\eta^{p^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\overline{U}^{E,\intercal}\Big(\widehat{\eta}^{i}_{t}-\eta^{\widehat{p}^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{\leq}U^{E,\intercal}\Big(\eta^{p^{i},\overline{r}^{i},\widehat{\pi}^{*,i}_{t}}-\eta^{p^{i},r^{i},\overline{\pi}^{i}}\Big)+cH\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{NT}{\delta}}{K}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+c^{\prime}H^{2}\sqrt{\frac{2}{n}\Big(\log\frac{SAHN}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\Big)}

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}LH\epsilon_{0}/2}+cH\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{NT}{\delta}}{K}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+c^{\prime}H^{2}\sqrt{\frac{2}{n}\Big(\log\frac{SAHN}{\delta}+(S-1)\log\big(e(1+n/(S-1))\big)\Big)},

where at (1) we have used the bounds derived earlier, and at (2) we have
applied Lemma
E.18
, noticing that we can choose policy
\overline{\pi}^{i}
as we wish,
and using that
k\leq\epsilon_{0}/2
.

∎

Lemma E.18
.

Let
\mathcal{M}_{1}=(\mathcal{S},\mathcal{A},H,s_{0},p,r^{1})
and

\mathcal{M}_{2}=(\mathcal{S},\mathcal{A},H,s_{0},p,r^{2})
be two MDPs with deterministic rewards
that differ only in the reward function
r^{1}\neq r^{2}
, and assume that, for
all
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\llbracket H\rrbracket
, it holds that
|r^{1}_{h}(s,a)-r^{2}_{h}(s,a)|\leq k
, for some

k\geq 0
. Let
\pi^{1}
be an arbitrary (potentially non-Markovian) policy that
induces, in
\mathcal{M}_{1}
, the distribution over returns
\eta^{p,r^{1},\pi^{1}}
. Then,
there exists a policy
\pi^{2}
that induces in
\mathcal{M}_{2}
the distribution

\eta^{p,r^{2},\pi^{2}}
such that:

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{U\in\underline{\mathfrak{U}}_{L}}\Big|\mathbb{E}_{G\sim\eta^{p,r^{1},\pi^{1}}}[U(G)]-\mathbb{E}_{G\sim\eta^{p,r^{2},\pi^{2}}}[U(G)]\Big|\leq LHk.

Proof.

A non-Markovian policy like
\pi^{1}
, in its most general form, prescribes
actions at stages
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket
depending on the sequence of
state-action-reward

(s_{1},a_{1},r_{1},s_{2},a_{2},r_{2},\dotsc,s_{h-1},a_{h-1},r_{h-1},s_{h})
received
so far. Since, by hypothesis, the reward functions are deterministic (see also
Section
2
), then it is clear that the information
contained in the rewards received so far (
\{r_{1},r_{2},\dotsc,r_{h-1}\}
) is
already contained in the state-action pairs received

(s_{1},a_{1},s_{2},a_{2},\dotsc,s_{h-1},a_{h-1},s_{h})
(indeed, for
deterministic reward
r^{1}
, we have that

r_{1}=r^{1}_{1}(s_{1},a_{1}),r_{2}=r^{1}_{2}(s_{2},a_{2})
, and so on). This means that, for any
non-Markovian policy in the MDP
\mathcal{M}_{1}
, since it coincides with
\mathcal{M}_{2}

except for the deterministic reward function, it is possible to construct a
policy
\pi^{2}
that induces the same distribution over
state-action

trajectories, i.e., for any state-action trajectory

\omega=(s_{1},a_{1},s_{2},a_{2},\dotsc,s_{H-1},a_{H-1},s_{H},a_{H},s_{H+1})\in\Omega
, it holds

\mathbb{P}_{p,r^{1},\pi^{1}}(\omega)=\mathbb{P}_{p,r^{2},\pi^{2}}(\omega)
.

Therefore, we can write:

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{U\in\underline{\mathfrak{U}}_{L}}\Big|\mathbb{E}_{G\sim\eta^{p,r^{1},\pi^{1}}}[U(G)]-\mathbb{E}_{G\sim\eta^{p,r^{2},\pi^{2}}}[U(G)]\Big|

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\sup\limits_{U\in\underline{\mathfrak{U}}_{L}}\Big|\sum\limits_{\omega\in\Omega}\mathbb{P}_{p,r^{1},\pi^{1}}(\omega)U\Big(\sum\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\omega}r^{1}_{h}(s,a)\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\sum\limits_{\omega\in\Omega}\mathbb{P}_{p,r^{2},\pi^{2}}(\omega)U\Big(\sum\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\omega}r^{2}_{h}(s,a)\Big)\Big|
